- **dev-python**: Use this agent for any Python work — libraries, CLIs, services, data processing, async code, packaging, typing. - **dev-review-audit**: The acceptance-audit review lens. Reads ONE change's diff, the full source of the changed files and the ticket it claims to implement, and returns findings as JSON, each with a verbatim evidence quote. Used by /dev-review to check the change against what was asked for, criterion by criterion. Judgement over bounded material, so it runs on the middle model. ([ayhid/claude-dev-workflow](https://github.com/ayhid/claude-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dev-review-audit.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **dev-review-blind**: The blind review lens. Reads ONE change's diff without being told what the change is for, and returns findings as JSON, each with a verbatim evidence quote. Used by /dev-review; it must run outside the session that knows the intent, because a reviewer who knows the intent reads code as confirmation of it. Judgement over bounded material, so it runs on the middle model. ([ayhid/claude-dev-workflow](https://github.com/ayhid/claude-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dev-review-blind.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **dev-review-edge**: The edge-case review lens. Reads ONE change's diff together with the full source of the changed files, and returns findings as JSON, each with a verbatim evidence quote. Used by /dev-review; the surrounding source is what makes an edge case real or not, since the patch alone does not show the guard three lines above the hunk. Judgement over bounded material, so it runs on the middle model. ([ayhid/claude-dev-workflow](https://github.com/ayhid/claude-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dev-review-edge.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **dev-sanity-llm**: Named teammate that runs dev sanity checks with an LLM. Escreve o código no padrão do projeto, com TDD nos dois Jest, e, em correção de bug, o teste de regressão que reproduz o erro. Trabalha no worktree e na branch da trilha, faz commit (e push quando houver remote), e não abre nem mergeia PR (isso é do chief, e o merge é do usuário). ([Faelst/fiap](https://github.com/Faelst/fiap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/developer.md))
- **developer**: Implements a single requirement handed over by Rumi (the product manager), in whatever project it's run in. ([farazfookeer/rumi-team](https://github.com/farazfookeer/rumi-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/rumi-team/agents/developer.md))
